Obertes al final del dia: 119 issues, 81 PR.
- Issues obertes avui (4): [#1032](https://github.com/tangency-labs/solidcore/issues/1032) perf: make SurfaceCache and CurveCache independent of the caller's handle type, so a shared cache is lent instead of copied; [#1018](https://github.com/tangency-labs/solidcore/issues/1018) Policy: backwards compatibility and behaviour vs SMLib (proposal: B2, per-fix runtime flags, SMLib = 1.0.0 default); [#960](https://github.com/tangency-labs/solidcore/issues/960) Cliff-edge overflow: a convex blend that runs past its faces is built as if they continued, and what it removes is subtracted; [#948](https://github.com/tangency-labs/solidcore/issues/948) SMLib boolean: difference of two capped ruled skins fails with SM_ERR (1001), no radial sector found (C++ and Rust alike)

## 7. Upstream i versions

crates.io: cap versió nova (última 1.0.1).
usd-brep: cap release nova (última v1.0.1; solidcore està fixat a vNone).
